{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1783483156-gmQZ6xStcVsbWOadDa3T","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_nhR6G9YQQZS3a3moOa7oXPo6","caller":{"type":"direct"},"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/prepare_event_announcement_hwpx.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Prepare an HWPX (zip) template by replacing {{placeholders}} in paragraph text.\n\nContract-specific behavior:\n- Placeholders may be split across multiple <hp:t> nodes inside a single <hp:p>.\n  We therefore concatenate all <hp:t> texts in a paragraph, do replacements on the\n  concatenated string, then put the final text into the first <hp:t> node and\n  clear remaining <hp:t> nodes.\n- If a paragraph is modified, remove any <hp:lineSegArray> (or case variants)\n  element from that <hp:p> to clear stale layout cache.\n- Repack as a valid zip (.hwpx). If a top-level 'mimetype' exists, store it\n  first uncompressed.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport shutil\nimport sys\nimport zipfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport xml.etree.ElementTree as ET\n\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"{{\\s*([^{}]+?)\\s*}}\")\n\n\ndef local_name(tag: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return localname of an ElementTree tag, lowercased.\"\"\"\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1].lower()\n    return tag.lower()\n\n\ndef load_event_data(json_path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:\n    with json_path.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError(f\"event data must be an object, got: {type(data)}\")\n    # Normalize to str->str\n    out: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        out[str(k)] = \"\" if v is None else str(v)\n    return out\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[str, set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Return (replaced_text, keys_used).\"\"\"\n    used: set[str] = set()\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key in mapping:\n            used.add(key)\n            return mapping[key]\n        return m.group(0)  # leave as-is\n\n    return PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text), used\n\n\ndef iter_xml_files(root_dir: Path):\n    for p in root_dir.rglob(\"*.xml\"):\n        if p.is_file():\n            yield p\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path: Path, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Process one XML file in-place. Returns stats.\"\"\"\n    raw = xml_path.read_bytes()\n\n    # Parse\n    try:\n        tree = ET.ElementTree(ET.fromstring(raw))\n    except ET.ParseError as e:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Failed to parse XML: {xml_path}: {e}\")\n\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    paragraphs_modified = 0\n    placeholders_used_total: set[str] = set()\n\n    # Find all paragraph elements regardless of namespace prefix.\n    for p in root.iter():\n        if local_name(p.tag) != \"p\":\n            continue\n\n        # Collect all <t> nodes inside this paragraph in document order\n        t_nodes = [n for n in p.iter() if local_name(n.tag) == \"t\"]\n        if not t_nodes:\n            continue\n\n        original_text = \"\".join((n.text or \"\") for n in t_nodes)\n        if \"{{\" not in original_text:\n            continue\n\n        replaced_text, used = replace_placeholders(original_text, mapping)\n        if replaced_text == original_text:\n            # No effective change (either no matching keys or identical replacement)\n            continue\n\n        placeholders_used_total |= used\n\n        # Put final text into first <t>, clear all remaining <t> nodes\n        t_nodes[0].text = replaced_text\n        for n in t_nodes[1:]:\n            n.text = \"\"\n\n        # Remove any lineSegArray element(s) from this paragraph (case-insensitive)\n        # ElementTree has no parent pointers, so we remove by scanning direct children.\n        removed_any = True\n        while removed_any:\n            removed_any = False\n            for child in list(p):\n                if local_name(child.tag) == \"linesegarray\":\n                    p.remove(child)\n                    removed_any = True\n\n        paragraphs_modified += 1\n\n    if paragraphs_modified:\n        # Write back, preserving XML declaration and UTF-8 encoding\n        tree.write(xml_path, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return {\n        \"paragraphs_modified\": paragraphs_modified,\n        \"placeholders_used\": len(placeholders_used_total),\n    }\n\n\ndef repack_hwpx(extracted_dir: Path, out_path: Path) -> None:\n    if out_path.exists():\n        out_path.unlink()\n\n    mimetype_path = extracted_dir / \"mimetype\"\n\n    def add_file(zf: zipfile.ZipFile, file_path: Path, arcname: str, compress_type: int):\n        info = zipfile.ZipInfo(arcname)\n        # Preserve a stable timestamp (ZipInfo date_time cannot be 0)\n        info.date_time = (1980, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0)\n        info.compress_type = compress_type\n        data = file_path.read_bytes()\n        zf.writestr(info, data)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(out_path, \"w\") as zf:\n        # No effective change (either no matching keys or identical replacement)\n100\t            continue\n101\t\n102\t        placeholders_used_total |= used\n103\t\n104\t        # Put final text into first <t>, clear all remaining <t> nodes\n105\t        t_nodes[0].text = replaced_text\n106\t        for n in t_nodes[1:]:\n107\t            n.text = \"\"\n108\t\n109\t        # Remove any lineSegArray element(s) from this paragraph (case-insensitive)\n110\t        # ElementTree has no parent pointers, so we remove by scanning direct children.\n111\t        removed_any = True\n112\t        while removed_any:\n113\t            removed_any = False\n114\t            for child in list(p):\n115\t                if local_name(child.tag) == \"linesegarray\":\n116\t                    p.remove(child)\n117\t                    removed_any = True\n118\t\n119\t        paragraphs_modified += 1\n120\t\n121\t    if paragraphs_modified:\n122\t        # Write back, preserving XML declaration and UTF-8 encoding\n123\t        tree.write(xml_path, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n124\t\n125\t    return {\n126\t        \"paragraphs_modified\": paragraphs_modified,\n127\t        \"placeholders_used\": len(placeholders_used_total),\n128\t    }\n129\t\n130\t\n131\tdef repack_hwpx(extracted_dir: Path, out_path: Path) -> None:\n132\t    if out_path.exists():\n133\t        out_path.unlink()\n134\t\n135\t    mimetype_path = extracted_dir / \"mimetype\"\n136\t\n137\t    def add_file(zf: zipfile.ZipFile, file_path: Path, arcname: str, compress_type: int):\n138\t        info = zipfile.ZipInfo(arcname)\n139\t        #
